## 11. Questions for the maintainer

1. **Nominatim policy tension.** The brief (l.193, l.318) mandates browser-direct calls that are "never cached or proxied by the API". The OSMF usage policy (<https://operations.osmfoundation.org/policies/nominatim/>) says "If at all possible, set up a proxy and also enable caching of requests", forbids client-side autocomplete (the brief already agrees: submit-only), requires an identifying Referer or User-Agent, and states that the public API may only be used "where the application developer has made a deliberate, informed decision to use it and is directly responsible for complying with this policy" (its "Usage in LLMs" section). Please confirm that browser-direct use is your deliberate decision, or approve a small caching proxy under `/api/v1/geocode` for M4. Until answered: browser-direct, submit-only, <= 1 req/s, Referer meta, attribution, disable toggle. Update 2026-09-09 (M4, verbatim from the policy page): "absolute maximum of 1 request per second"; "Provide a valid HTTP Referer or User-Agent identifying the application"; "If at all possible, set up a proxy and also enable caching of requests"; "Auto-complete search ... you must not implement such a service on the client side using the API"; "LLMs may only suggest this service, if they prominently point to this usage policy and explain the restrictions of use to the user"; the API "must not be built into, offered through, suggested by, or automatically generated by no-code, low-code, or vibe-coding platforms as a generic geocoding ... service". This project was assembled with an LLM under your direction: please read the policy and confirm the deliberate decision (browser-direct as the brief mandates, or the `/api/v1/geocode` proxy) before production use. M4 implements the brief (D97).
2. **HYG version.** The brief pins v4.2; the repository now serves v4.4 at `data/hyg/CURRENT/` (v4.2 remains at `data/hyg/OLDER/`). v4.4 merged duplicated Gliese-Jahreiss stars and relabelled two stars, which can change the HIP-to-name crosswalk row count. Default: v4.4 with the attribution naming it.
3. **Stellarium figure set.** The brief chooses `modern`; Skyfield 1.55's `parse_constellations_json` docstring names `modern_st` (Sky & Telescope figures). Both parse identically. Default: `modern`, with a code comment so nobody "fixes" it.
4. **Playwright system libraries.** Browsers cannot launch on this WSL until `sudo env "PATH=$PATH" fnm exec --using=24 npm --prefix frontend exec -- playwright install-deps` (or the equivalent `apt install`) runs once; sudo is outside the agent's remit. Until then `make e2e` is blocked locally (CI is unaffected). Update 2026-09-06: a sudo-free alternative works: the Debian packages extracted into `~/.local/pw-libs` and exported through `LD_LIBRARY_PATH`/`FONTCONFIG_PATH` (docs/dev-wsl2.md) ran the suite locally (8/8 on `chromium-desktop` and `chromium-mobile`); `install-deps` stays the supported path (B-24).
5. **TypeScript 7.1 timing.** TS 7.1 (targeted autumn 2026) should ship the API that unblocks typescript-eslint. Do you want the alias recipe (`npm:@typescript/typescript6`) attempted at M6, or only a plain bump once typescript-eslint and openapi-typescript declare support?
6. **`backend/data/` packaging.** The brief puts the committed CSV/TOML under `backend/data/` (l.354), outside `src/`, so `uv_build` does not package it. Options at M1: keep the path and COPY it in the Dockerfile with a settings entry, or move it under `src/skyapi/data/` (deviation from the layout). Default: decide at M1, preferring the move if the Dockerfile would otherwise need a second data path.
7. **Sun GM constant.** The brief names `GM_SUN` (l.316) and `GM_SUN_Pitjeva_2005_km3_s2` (l.530); Skyfield 1.55 has no `GM_SUN` and its documentation aliases `GM_SUN_DE440_km3_s2`. Default: the DE440 value, consistent with the ephemeris.
8. **Serpens boundary shape.** The contract (l.149) has one `boundary` polygon per constellation, but the IAU defines Serpens as two disjoint polygons. M1 keeps the contract shape and adds an optional `boundary_parts` list (present only for Ser), which enters `openapi.json` and the generated types at M2 under semver of `api_version`. Alternative: make `boundary` a list of polygons for every constellation. Default: the additive field.
9. **Magnitude formulas.** Skyfield's `planetary_magnitude` covers Mercury to Neptune only; the Sun, Moon and Pluto use cited textbook formulas (Sun `-26.74 + 5 log10 d`, Moon Allen's phase law, Pluto `-1.0 + 5 log10(r Delta)`), asteroids the H-G law, comets `g + 5 log10 Delta + 2.5 k log10 r`. Conformance tolerance against Horizons: 0.5 mag, display-only. Please confirm or name a preferred source.
10. **`make data` scope.** It runs `sky-data fetch` and then `sky-data build-caches` (B-30) so a fresh clone is ready in one command; the brief's wording is fetch only. Keep?
11. **Rotation model for planetary observers.** Skyfield cannot build planet frames from the text PCK, so `astro/frames.py` implements the IAU model of `pck00011.tpc` (IAU 2015, ADR-0007). The Horizons manual says planets use the IAU 2009 models, but the fixtures show Horizons' Mars pole equals the pck00011 pole within 0.017 arcsec at every epoch, and Jezero alt/az agree within 0.0006 arcsec. No decision needed unless another body disagrees when M2 adds observers beyond the fixtures; `pck00010.tpc` (IAU 2009) stays an optional registry entry.
12. **Minor-body defaults never contain a comet.** Brief l.155 ranks `/minor-bodies/defaults` as asteroids with H <= 9 sorted by H, then comets; 6,795 asteroids have H <= 9, so with `max_minor_bodies = 100` the list is always the 100 lowest-H asteroids and comets never appear (they remain searchable, and Pluto (134340) is excluded from the defaults because it is a major body). Default: keep the brief's ranking. Alternative: reserve a share of the cap for comets whose epoch lies within 2 years.
13. **`/health` while starting.** The contract (l.112-113) lists `status`, `progress?`, `version`; M2 answers 503 with `Retry-After` while `starting` (so the Docker healthcheck fails until the data is loaded) and adds the optional `missing` (group codes when degraded) and `detail` (fatal reason) fields (B-41). Confirm, or prefer 200 with the status in the body only.
14. **`/meta.catalogs` when degraded.** The `dso`, `constellations` and `minor_bodies` entries are absent while their data is missing (B-42), and the frontend must treat them as optional. Alternative: always present with zero counts and empty ETags.
15. **`step_class` on `/meta.bodies[]`.** Added (B-43) so the frontend clamps `step_s` from data instead of hard-coding which body belongs to which `max_step_s` class (l.70, l.127). Keep?
16. **Headless WebGPU in CI.** The `chromium-webgpu` Playwright project (full Chromium, `--enable-unsafe-webgpu --use-webgpu-adapter=swiftshader`) is configured but not in the CI run list. Measured on this arm64 WSL (Chromium 153): the SwiftShader adapter and device are created, but configuring the canvas context loses the device, so the spec crashes; the WGSL path passes in Brave on the Windows GPU (60.3 fps). After the first push, please run `npm --prefix frontend run e2e -- --project=chromium-webgpu` once on the x64 runner (a one-off workflow step) and report; if the x64 SwiftShader build presents correctly, the project joins the CI list.
17. **Ecliptic epoch.** The frontend draws the J2000 ecliptic from the constant obliquity (B-54). If the ecliptic of date is wanted, the backend would add an `ecliptic_of_date.q` series to `/sky/frame` under semver of `api_version`.
18. **Vitest 5 deferral.** Vitest 5.0.0 is the latest release but `@testing-library/jest-dom` 7.0.1 loses its matcher types under it (#738), so M3 stays on 4.1.11 with `vitest` pinned exact (B-49, B-55). Bump both packages once jest-dom ships a fix, or say if dropping jest-dom is preferable.

19. **English constellation labels.** The frontend shows the Latin names as the English labels ("Canis Major", as English sky charts and Stellarium do) and keeps the `english` gloss of `constellation_names.csv` ("Great Dog") for the details panel (Q39). Say if the gloss should be the label instead.
20. **Geolocation prompt timing.** OBS-1 says "after a one-line explanation"; M4 opens the browser prompt on the first tick with the explanation visible beside it (B-69), plus a "Use my location" button. Prefer a click-gated prompt?
21. **`/sky/altaz.constellation`.** M4 adds this additive optional field (Skyfield's bundled B1875 constellation map) and bumps `api_version` to 1.1.0 (B-71) so the client never tests the precessed boundary polygons itself. Confirm the contract touch.
22. **Night brightness in the URL.** The `night` parameter accepts a decimal level (`night=0.6`, B-59, Q38). Keep, or leave the brightness ephemeral?
23. **Accessibility tooling.** `@axe-core/playwright` would need an ADR (brief l.405); M4 ships a hand-written `a11y.spec.ts` and Lighthouse >= 90 is measured at M6. Approve the dependency for M6? Update 2026-09-24 (M6, decide-and-continue): `@axe-core/playwright ^4.13.0` added under ADR-0020 (MPL-2.0; axe-core 4.13.0 was already in `node_modules` through `eslint-plugin-jsx-a11y`); Lighthouse 13.5.0 runs from `scripts/lighthouse.mjs` through the exact-pinned `npm --prefix frontend exec --package=lighthouse@13.5.0` (its 116 packages / 183 MB are not in the lockfile); the six URLs score 1.00. Say if a devDependency is preferred (a one-line change).
24. **Planetary site presets.** Coordinates live in `frontend/src/state/presets.ts` with Gazetteer citations and a `gazetteer` registry entry instead of `backend/src/skyapi/data/planetary_sites.csv` (B-57). Keep?

25. **M5 device checklist.** `docs/testing.md` "Manual checks" holds the numbered augmented-reality checklist for iOS Safari 17+ and Android Chrome 120+ (permissions, compass accuracy, calibration drag, camera field against the Moon, time badge, exit and camera light, denied paths, WebXR entry/north/exit, a phone without ARCore, Mars exit). Please run it on real devices and record the rows (the l.478 flag). Update 2026-09-24 (M6): unchanged, and the M6 budgets table now carries the phone rows the checklist must fill: `>= 30 fps` on a 2022 mid-range phone with every layer on (`#selftest` at the default cap 2, then with `#dpr=1.5`; the Q57 rule of D142 decides the cap), `selfTest().staleMs` (the l.264 live drift on the device), the browser matrix rows for iOS Safari 17+ and Android Chrome 120+ (D167). The host-GPU rows (Brave on Windows) and the CI rows (Chromium 153) are recorded; Edge, Firefox and Safari rows are yours too.
26. **WebGPU phones and WebXR.** Babylon 9.25 enters an `immersive-ar` session on the WebGL2 engine only (Chrome's WebXR/WebGPU binding is flag-only), so a WebGPU phone gets the sensor mode and no WebXR switch (B-79, D129). Keep WebGPU there (architecture l.87), or prefer WebGL2 at engine creation when `isSessionSupported('immersive-ar')` is true so ARCore phones get the [S] feature? Update 2026-09-24 (M6): unchanged; the plan proceeded with the recorded default (WebGL2-only WebXR, B-79); `XrBridge.test.ts` (B-93) now proves the bridge on `NullEngine`, the device rows stay with question 25.
27. **Star and deep-sky blending.** The star and DSO shaders now write coverage alpha and blend with `ALPHA_PREMULTIPLIED_PORTERDUFF` in every mode (D122): defined compositing over the camera video and in the PNG export, identical for isolated pixels on the black sky, star crossovers slightly less bright. Accept, or prefer the AR-only `uAr` switch (identical to M4 outside AR)? Update 2026-09-24 (M6): unchanged; the M6 pixel probes, the Lighthouse night runs and the host-GPU rows were measured with the coverage-alpha blend (the recorded default).
28. **Phone TLS.** `@vitejs/plugin-basic-ssl` (l.548) issues a non-CA leaf with a DNS-only SAN, which no phone can trust for a LAN IP; the documented paths are Chrome port forwarding of the plain-HTTP preview (Android) and a mkcert CA with `DEV_TLS_CERT`/`DEV_TLS_KEY` (iOS) (B-82, D135). Accept, and is a `make dev-phone` recipe wanted? Update 2026-09-24 (M6): unchanged (the recorded default, ADR-0021); the dev server now serves a per-process CSP nonce; with `worker-src blob:` in the dev policy the tab reconnects and reloads itself with the new nonce after a restart (R103, closed at Step F), unless the phone has not accepted the dev certificate for the restart poller's WebSocket, in which case it stays on `Polling for restart...` and needs a manual reload; and `SKY_GEOCODER_ORIGIN` is read from the repository-root `.env` when serving pages (D148).

29. **Cold 4G reading and the bright-prefix loader.** "A cold 4G connection" (l.257) reads as DevTools "Fast 4G" (9 Mbps, 165 ms) at M6; the measured cold boot is 5.63 s there (5.27 s on WebPageTest's 4G profile, 27.2 s under Lighthouse's "Slow 4G"), against the 5 s budget (B-84). Options: accept the deviation and rely on nginx `gzip_static` of the SKYS at M7 (about 4.9-5.2 s, marginal); or approve the bright-prefix loader over multipart HTTP Range (mag <= 7.0, 15,537 rows, 497 kB, about 2.2 s cold; contract-neutral; a warm boot pays the 0.5 MB prefix again because Chromium bypasses its cache on multi-range requests; exclusive with nginx compressing the SKYS). Default until answered: B-84. Update 2026-09-28 (M7, B-99, D180): the edge cannot gzip a proxied response, so the first option reads as an API-side stored gzip of the SKYS (R115), never nginx `gzip_static`.
30. **French typography.** 52 strings gained U+00A0 before `:` and `;`, `time.local` became "Heure locale", `boot.downloading` reads `({{percent}} %)`, and every number placeholder followed by a unit carries U+00A0 in both languages; apostrophes stay straight (no U+2019). Please review the edited strings; `scripts/check_i18n.mjs` enforces the convention from M6 (D158).
31. **Phone sheet and keyboard focus.** At 320 px a keyboard user tabbing into the top bar with the sheet open focused seven entirely hidden controls (WCAG 2.2 SC 2.4.11); M6 collapses the sheet only on a keyboard (`:focus-visible`) focus landing on a top-bar control the sheet covers, never on a tap, and caps the whole sheet at 70 dvh (D157). Alternative: a measured `max-h` cap alone that keeps the sheet open. Accept?
32. **AR nudge direction.** The two 1° buttons beside the AR offset badge (SC 2.5.7, the non-drag twin of the calibration drag) "turn the sky": the right button moves the drawn sky to the right like a rightward drag does (`nudgeArOffset(-1)`), the left one the other way (D157 C5, Q85). If "turn the view" is preferred, the two signs and their assertions swap.
33. **Text-field border contrast.** `TextField` draws its border as `border-muted/80` in every scheme since M6 (`/60` gave 2.27:1 in the light scheme and 2.51:1 in night mode, below the 3:1 of SC 1.4.11; `/80` gives 3.52-5.32:1) and `styles/tokens.test.ts` parses that alpha from the source (D157 C8, Q86). A light-and-night-only rule would leave the dark scheme at 3.37:1. Accept `/80` everywhere?
34. **Switch state word.** The "On"/"Off" text of every `Switch` now sits outside the button as an `aria-hidden` sibling, so the accessible name is the label alone (SC 2.5.3 label in name; axe counts `aria-hidden` text inside a control as visible) and the word is no longer part of the tap target (D157 C4, Q87). Accept, or prefer the word inside the control with the name extended to include it?
35. **Web base image.** The web serving stage is `nginx:stable-alpine-slim`, not the brief's `nginx:stable-alpine` (l.35): the brief's tag measured 66,039,808 B unpacked (25,981,205 B compressed) on 2026-09-28, over the 50 MB budget of l.299 before a single asset, while slim measures 15,212,544 B (6,103,396 B) and keeps nginx 1.30.5, the envsubst entrypoint, `wget --spider` and `gzip_static` (B-98, D175). The reversal rule (the brief's tag under 40 MB unpacked) did not fire. Accept slim?
36. **The api image against 400 MB.** Answered by measurement on 2026-09-28: after the ladder rungs 2-5 (pip and ensurepip, the vendored test suites, the unused pyarrow components, `idlelib`/`tkinter`/`turtledemo`) the api image is 394,364,527 B unpacked on arm64 (98.6 % of the budget) and 379,547,713 B on amd64 (a QEMU build here; the runner's own figure follows the push), so B-111 did not trigger and no decision is needed. Kept for the record: had either figure exceeded 400,000,000 B, the options were the compressed reading (B-111, `--api-reading compressed`), dropping bytecode (l.489) or an alpine Python (l.35). The 5.6 MB of slack on arm64 will shrink with a dependency refresh: say if a compressed reading would be acceptable then.
37. **Root-master nginx.** The web container runs the official model (a root master, uid-101 workers, `cap_add: [CHOWN, SETGID, SETUID, NET_BIND_SERVICE, KILL]` under `cap_drop: [ALL]`) rather than `nginxinc/nginx-unprivileged` (D175, D187). Keep, or prefer the unprivileged image (ports above 1024, a different template entrypoint)?
38. **QEMU registration.** The agent registered no binfmt handler; Docker Desktop's own restart on 2026-09-28 (after the daemon outage) registered `qemu-x86_64`, so the amd64 images were built here under emulation and measured (both under budget). Under QEMU `import pyarrow` and `import pandas` segfault whatever `ARROW_USER_SIMD_LEVEL` (an emulator limitation), so the amd64 runtime proof is the CI `docker` job's after your push (D178). Is that division acceptable, or do you want a native amd64 build machine in the loop?
39. **HSTS value.** nginx in tls mode sends `Strict-Transport-Security: max-age=31536000; includeSubDomains` on TLS connections only, never `preload` (D181). Keep 31536000, or 63072000 (two years)? `includeSubDomains` assumes no plain-HTTP subdomain; say if one exists.
40. **TLS termination.** Path A: nginx terminates (`SKY_WEB_MODE=tls`, `compose.tls.yaml`, certificates in `SKY_TLS_DIR`); path B: an external proxy (http mode, `web` narrowed to loopback through `compose.override.yaml`, the proxy owning HSTS and appended to `FORWARDED_ALLOW_IPS` when it is off the compose network) (D182). Which one?
41. **Certificates.** The dotfile rule `location ~ /\. { return 404; }` blocks HTTP-01 under `/.well-known/`; the runbook assumes DNS-01 or an external ACME client, or an explicit `location ^~ /.well-known/acme-challenge/` before the dotfile rule (R133, checklist item 3). No OCSP stapling. Which renewal path, and is stapling wanted?
42. **`Server: nginx`.** OSS nginx cannot omit the `Server` header; `server_tokens off` hides the version and `proxy_hide_header Server` keeps `Server: uvicorn` from the client (D185, D193). Acceptable, or is the commercial `server_tokens` string variant wanted?
43. **Edge error log level.** `error_log crit` at http level (request-bound error lines carry the client address and the query string; l.275) hides upstream diagnostics such as a resolver failure (D184, R130). Keep `crit` for privacy, or `error` for the first weeks of production?
44. **The compose subnet.** `10.213.0.0/24` is written once (`x-sky.subnet`) and used as the network and as `FORWARDED_ALLOW_IPS` (D188); it sits outside Docker's default pools and WSL 2's NAT range here. Does it collide with anything on the production host (`Pool overlaps` -> `compose.override.yaml`)? Update 2026-09-28 (the fix pass): `FORWARDED_ALLOW_IPS` is `web`'s fixed address `10.213.0.3` (`x-sky.web_address`), not the network; a collision changes the subnet, the address and the variable together in `compose.override.yaml`.
45. **uid policy.** The api image runs as 1000:1000 (the development host's uid) and compose exposes `SKY_UID`/`SKY_GID` for other hosts (D170, D194), so the data directory must be owned by that pair. Keep, or prefer a documented `chown` to a fixed service account?
46. **Memory limit.** `SKY_API_MEMORY_LIMIT` defaults to 2G under the rule 1 GB plus 1 GB per worker (R114: a worker serving concurrent 100-body frames peaks near 1 GB) (D187). The first-start `memory.peak` of the acceptance run is recorded in `docs/testing.md` "Delivery (M7)"; confirm the rule for your worker count.
47. **`SKYAPI_AUTO_FETCH` in production.** `false` with the out-of-band `docker compose run --rm api sky-data fetch` and the weekly `update` cron (the documented order), or `true` accepting a download at a restart after a registry change (a 3.3 GB de441 download outlives the 300 s start period: R120)? Checklist item 8.
48. **Derive `SKYAPI_GEOCODER_URL` from `SKY_GEOCODER_ORIGIN` in compose?** The two must agree (R104, checklist item 1); compose could set the API's URL from the web tier's origin and close the drift by construction. Default no (the API's variable stays the operator's).
49. **Production values.** The domain, `SKYAPI_CORS_ORIGINS` (the app origin; the development `https://localhost:5173` must not ship), `SKYAPI_GEOCODER_EMAIL` (public by design), `SKYAPI_WORKERS` (the cores) and `SKY_API_MEMORY_LIMIT` are yours to set in `.env` before the first production `up`.
50. **Log rotation.** Both services use the json-file driver capped at `10m` x `3` (D184, D187). Enough, or is the host's logging driver (journald, a syslog collector) preferred? Checklist item 16.
51. **Image labels from `make up`.** `GIT_SHA`/`BUILD_DATE` are `unknown` in local builds and set by CI (`GIT_SHA=${{ github.sha }}`) (Q104). Should `make up` pass them too?
52. **Docker Hub pull limits in CI.** The `docker` job pulls `python:3.14-slim`, `node:24` and `nginx:stable-alpine-slim` anonymously from Docker Hub (hadolint comes from ghcr). If the first remote runs flake on the anonymous limit, a Docker Hub login token as a repository secret is the fix (R144).
53. **Keep hadolint after the first remote run?** hadolint 2.15.1 runs as a pinned image in CI and `make images` (B-106, D177). Keep it as a permanent gate, or drop it after the first green remote run?
54. **The editor's TT - UT at far dates (B-112).** Typing a wall time for antiquity lands the clock hours off (3 h 00 min 50 s for 15 March -44 on de441), because the editor converts with the current window's TT - UT. Options: a `/sky/frame` round trip at the typed instant before applying (no contract change, one extra request, the dialog waits), a `tt_minus_ut` query parameter on `/meta` or `/sky/altaz` (an additive contract change, `api_version` 1.2.0), or accepting the offset with the readout as the truth (the URL and the readout are consistent today). Default: the frame round trip, post-M7.

Human-only items after M6 and M7 (the agent cannot run them; the approved plans' section 6 lists, `docs/testing.md` "Manual checks", "Accessibility audit" and "Delivery (M7)", the release checklist of `docs/architecture.md` "Deployment") (M7, 2026-09-28: retitled from "Human-only items after M6"; items 9-12 added; items 4 and 7 carry dated notes):

1. The phone protocol of `docs/testing.md` "Manual checks" on a Pixel 6a-class Android phone and an iPhone: the all-layers URL with `#selftest` at the default cap 2, then with `dpr=1.5`, then the AR checklist rows; the Q57 decision on the coarse-pointer DPR cap follows the four `fps` figures (D142; question 25 row 13).
2. The host-browser rows for Firefox on Windows (not installed on this host) and Safari on a Mac (D167; the l.268 row of the budgets table); Edge 154 was measured by the agent on 2026-09-27 (WebGPU 60.0 / 59.9 fps, WebGL2 59.7 fps, app-mode windows) and is in that row.
3. The `webkit` and `chromium-webgpu` projects on the x86-64 runner after the first push (B-51, R112).
4. `git push` and the first remote CI run: the Lighthouse step on x86-64 (R100) and the weekly `audit` job (R105). (M7, 2026-09-28: the same push now also runs the `docker` job, item 9.)
5. Done by the agent on 2026-09-27: `sky-data fetch --full` resumed and completed `de441.bsp` (3,307,878,400 B, SHA-256 verified) and the de441 ready-time (4.69 s) and RSS rows (`VmHWM` 866.4 MB, `RssAnon + RssShmem` 748.6-760.1 MB, `RssFile` 66-69 MB) are in the budgets table; nothing left here.
6. Lighthouse's ten manual audits and a screen-reader pass (VoiceOver, TalkBack) including the AR overlay.

The M7 decisions handed over (`docs/architecture.md` "Security and privacy", the M7 item of section 6): HSTS ownership and `preload`, the production `SKY_GEOCODER_ORIGIN`, `FORWARDED_ALLOW_IPS`, the nginx `location` blocks (`/api/v1/docs`, `.vite/`), SKYS compression versus byte ranges. (Done at M7, 2026-09-28: every hand-off item has its outcome under section 6 "M7 Delivery", Amended items; what remains is yours: questions 35-53, items 10-12.)
8. Answers to questions 1, 23 and 25-34.
9. M7: `git push` and the first remote CI run of the M7 commits: the `docker` job builds the linux/amd64 images on the runner (`ubuntu-latest`, Docker 28.0.4) and prints their `check_images.mjs` record lines (the amd64 export payloads of record; the QEMU figures measured here are 379,547,713 B api and 16,375,691 B web), `docker build --check` on the runner's Docker (dropped from CI if that version lacks it, kept in `make images`), the e2e job's Lighthouse step, the weekly `audit` job, then the `webkit` and `chromium-webgpu` one-offs of item 3 (checklist item 18; R100, R105, R122).
10. M7: real certificates and the TLS deployment choice: nginx in tls mode (`SKY_WEB_MODE=tls`, `compose.tls.yaml`, `fullchain.pem` and `privkey.pem` in `SKY_TLS_DIR`) or an external proxy (http mode, `compose.override.yaml`, the proxy owning HSTS and appended to `FORWARDED_ALLOW_IPS` when off the compose network); the renewal path (DNS-01 or an explicit ACME location) and the HSTS value (questions 39-41; checklist items 2-3).

M7: the production `.env` values (`SKYAPI_EPHEMERIS=de441.bsp` with `sky-data fetch --full` done, `SKYAPI_WORKERS` = the cores and `SKY_API_MEMORY_LIMIT` = 1 GB + 1 GB per worker, `SKYAPI_CORS_ORIGINS` = the app origin, a public `SKYAPI_GEOCODER_EMAIL`, `SKY_GEOCODER_ORIGIN` equal to the origin of `SKYAPI_GEOCODER_URL`, the `SKYAPI_AUTO_FETCH` policy, the data directory owned by `SKY_UID:SKY_GID`), the weekly cron in its compose form, and the Nominatim decision of question 1 before production (checklist items 1, 4-11, 15, 17; questions 46-50).

(1) `lang` was omitted from the URL at its default `en` while a URL without it takes the language of `navigator.languages`, so on a browser preferring French an explicit English was lost on reload and in the copied link (OBS-8 l.197, UX-2 l.246, acceptance l.574): `lang` joined the always-written keys (D79, D109 and Q32 amended, ADR-0012 amendment), with the rule in `url.test.ts`, a lang-completion case in `urlSync.test.ts` and a `url-state.spec.ts` test under `locale: 'fr-FR'` (the copied link opened afresh, a reload, the way back to French) that fails on the old serializer (`Expected: "en", Received: null`). (2) The year field of the time editor and the elevation field asked for a numeric or decimal keypad, which has no minus key on phones (l.575, TIME-2): both take `inputMode="text"`, asserted by their component tests, and row 25 of the device checklist asks the maintainer to type a negative year and elevation on a phone. (3) The Nominatim attribution read "(c) OpenStreetMap contributors" where the brief mandates "© OpenStreetMap contributors" (l.193, l.318): the registry, the OpenAPI example and the four generated files carry the sign, pinned by `test_registry.py`, `test_meta.py`, `PlaceSearch.test.tsx`, `observer.spec.ts` and `about.spec.ts`; `api_version` stays 1.1.0 (an `examples` string is not a contract change: ADR-0017 amendment, `.claude/rules/tooling.md`).
